*Derek Muller spends twenty-seven minutes counting microstates on screen --- more commitment to the bit than most textbooks ever manage.*

[]{#glEvent .anchor}**Event Horizon** --- The surface of a black hole: the boundary past which nothing, light included, can return to a distant observer. It is a boundary for distant observers, not a steel lid: nothing piles up against it like post at a closed office. (Chapter 6)

**PBS Space Time.** ["What Happens at the Event Horizon? \| Space Time \| PBS Digital Studios"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mht-1c4wc0Q) YouTube, 8 Dec. 2016, youtube.com/watch?v=mht-1c4wc0Q.

*Eleven minutes at the edge of no return --- narrated by an astrophysicist who treats spaghettification as a Tuesday-afternoon inconvenience.*

[]{#glFineStructure .anchor}**Fine-Structure Constant --- The dimensionless number (α ≈ 1/137.036) that fixes how strongly an electron and a photon interact. Measured to about ten significant figures; never derived from anything more basic. (Backlog)**

**PBS Space Time.** ["Why Is 1/137 One of the Greatest Unsolved Problems In Physics?"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RCSSgxV9qNw) YouTube, 28 Sept. 2022, youtube.com/watch?v=RCSSgxV9qNw.

*Sixteen minutes on why 1/137 refuses to explain itself, delivered by a host who seems personally offended by the number's stubbornness.*

[]{#glHawking .anchor}**Hawking Radiation** --- The faint thermal radiation a black hole emits by quantum processes, causing it to lose mass. Smaller black holes are hotter and evaporate faster. (Chapter 6)

**PBS Space Time.** ["Hawking Radiation"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qPKj0YnKANw) YouTube, 15 Mar. 2018, youtube.com/watch?v=qPKj0YnKANw.

*Twelve minutes of pair production at the horizon, delivered by an astrophysicist who says 'virtual particle' without breaking stride. Same video as Black Hole, which covers what a horizon actually is.*

[]{#glHidden .anchor}**Hidden Variables** --- Proposed extra properties a system carries in advance that would fix measurement outcomes. Bell's theorem rules out the local kind; pilot-wave theory is a surviving nonlocal kind. (Chapter 13)

**Sixty Symbols.** ["Hidden Variables (extra) - Sixty Symbols"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=t35Q7C-DhaU) YouTube, 12 Oct. 2022, youtube.com/watch?v=t35Q7C-DhaU.

*Seven minutes of Bell's inequality, hosted by a physicist who trusts the maths over intuition.*

**Hilbert space** --- The mathematical room in which quantum states live as vectors. Superposition is addition in that room. It is not a building in Coldharrow, though several clerks have asked. (Chapter 1)

**Quantum Sense.** ["Ch 6: What are bras and bra-ket notation? \| Maths of Quantum Mechanics"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=lRR-qgjaKlg) YouTube, 10 Jan. 2023, youtube.com/watch?v=lRR-qgjaKlg.

*Same film as Bra--Ket Notation; the room the brackets were always describing.*

**Holographic Duality (sometimes labelled AdS/CFT) --- A fully worked example of holography: gravity in a curved box of space is exactly equivalent to an ordinary quantum theory, with no gravity, living only on that box's boundary. Same physics, two descriptions; the boundary carries the whole story. See also Holographic Principle, the general claim this entry makes good on. (Chapter 14)**

**PBS Space Time.** ["Does Space Emerge From A Holographic Boundary?"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DoCYY9sa2kU) YouTube, 21 Mar. 2024, youtube.com/watch?v=DoCYY9sa2kU.

*The full Maldacena conjecture in one sitting --- twenty-odd minutes of snow-globe analogies from a host who doesn't flinch at the boundary integral.*

[]{#glHolographicPrinciple .anchor}**Holographic Principle --- The proposal that everything happening inside a region of space, gravity included, can be fully described by information on its lower-dimensional boundary. See also Holographic Duality, the one fully worked example of it. (Chapter 14)**

**PBS Space Time.** ["The Holographic Universe Explained"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=klpDHn8viX8) YouTube, 10 Apr. 2019, youtube.com/watch?v=klpDHn8viX8.

*Fourteen minutes on the holographic argument, standing in for a chapter, hosted by an astrophysicist unbothered by AdS/CFT.*

[]{#glInformation .anchor}**Information Problem (black-hole)** --- The unresolved question of what becomes of the detailed quantum information in everything a black hole swallows, given that its radiation looks merely thermal. Most physicists expect the information survives. (Chapter 6)

**PBS Space Time.** ["Have We SOLVED The Black Hole Information Paradox with Wormholes?"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=epSev7ovVew) YouTube, 15 June 2022, youtube.com/watch?v=epSev7ovVew.

*Fourteen minutes of wormholes and the replica trick, hosted by an astrophysicist who treats Hawking's paradox as solvable homework, not existential dread.*

[]{#glInterference .anchor}**Interference** --- The reinforcing or cancelling of quantum alternatives that have not been recorded, and the observable sign that a superposition is more than ordinary ignorance. (Chapter 1)

**PBS Space Time.** ["How Decoherence Splits The Quantum Multiverse"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GlOwJWJWPUs) YouTube, 24 Feb. 2020, youtube.com/watch?v=GlOwJWJWPUs.

*Fifteen minutes with a working astrophysicist on why interference dies the moment the universe quietly records which path you took. Same video as Decoherence, which covers where the leaked information goes.*

[]{#glLandauers .anchor}**Landauer's Principle --- The result that erasing one bit of information has a minimum unavoidable energy cost, released as heat. The genuine, narrow link between information and thermodynamics. See Chapter 16's aside on Maxwell's demon, ancestor of every clerk who thinks information is free. (Chapter 16)**

**Up and Atom.** ["Pure Information Gives Off Heat"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XY-mbr-aAZE) YouTube, 29 July 2022, youtube.com/watch?v=XY-mbr-aAZE.

*Jade takes the paperwork's thought experiment and actually draws the bit-flipping diagrams, so you can watch the heat you were merely warned about.*

[]{#glLocality .anchor}**Locality** --- The assumption that what is true here cannot depend on a distant, freely chosen measurement except through influences no faster than light. Bell's theorem shows nature does not respect it in the way local hidden variables would require. (Chapter 13)

**Veritasium.** ["There Is Something Faster Than Light"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=NIk_0AW5hFU) YouTube, 25 Dec. 2025, youtube.com/watch?v=NIk_0AW5hFU.

*Same locality problem restaged with lasers and a straight face --- Veritasium giving real screen time to the deeper free-choice loophole physicists still argue about, distinct from the detection and locality loopholes closed in 2015.*

[]{#glMtheory .anchor}**M-theory** --- The still-incomplete eleven-dimensional framework in which the five string theories are one structure seen five ways, and the fundamental objects include membranes as well as strings. (Chapter 14)

**ScienceClic English.** ["M Theory \| Towards a theory of everything?"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=plU31-qtZ78) YouTube, 6 Dec. 2023, youtube.com/watch?v=plU31-qtZ78.

*Seventeen minutes of patiently rendered eleven-dimensional geometry, walking through all five string theories while staying upfront that M-theory itself isn't finished. Same video as Duality; this entry is the membranes-and-branes half.*

[]{#glMany .anchor}**Many-Worlds (Everett) Interpretation** --- The view that the smooth quantum law never breaks down and every measurement-like interaction branches the universe, all outcomes occurring in branches that no longer interact. No collapse; unlimited branches. (Chapter 11)

**PBS Space Time.** ["Where Are The Worlds In Many Worlds?"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=z-syaCoqkZA) YouTube, 13 Jul. 2021, youtube.com/watch?v=z-syaCoqkZA.

*Matt O'Dowd goes looking for the other branches in thirteen minutes, and reports, not unreasonably, that they have stopped returning our calls.*

[]{#glMatter .anchor}**Matter Wave --- The wave aspect of material particles --- the reason electrons produce diffraction and interference patterns. (Chapter 12)**

**Sixty Symbols.** ["de Broglie Waves - Sixty Symbols"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JIGI-eXK0tg) YouTube, 17 May 2011, youtube.com/watch?v=JIGI-eXK0tg.

*De Broglie's matter waves explained in under seven minutes by a Nottingham physicist who isn't afraid of the maths.*

[]{#glMeasurement .anchor}**Measurement** --- In physics, any physical interaction that records or spreads information about an outcome. Not an act of attention: no mind, eye or clipboard required. (Chapter 1)

**Sixty Symbols.** ["Quantum Physics Woo - Sixty Symbols"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8DGgvE6hLAU) YouTube, 4 Dec. 2013, youtube.com/watch?v=8DGgvE6hLAU.

*A working physicist reads a tabloid's 'quantum consciousness' headline aloud, then dismantles it --- measurement, it turns out, was never watching for applause.*

[]{#glMeasurementBasis .anchor}**Measurement Basis** --- The particular set of alternatives a given apparatus can tell apart. For incompatible quantum properties there is no basis-independent set of true values waiting to be read off. (Chapter 2)

**3Blue1Brown.** ["Some light quantum mechanics (with minutephysics)"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MzRCDLre1b4) YouTube, 13 Sept. 2017, youtube.com/watch?v=MzRCDLre1b4.

*Grant Sanderson and Henry Reich run three polarising filters past a beam of light and make you watch the measurement basis change the answer in real time.*

[]{#glMeasurementProblem .anchor}**Measurement Problem** --- The open question of what, if anything, turns the smooth quantum description into the single definite outcome an experiment records. (Chapter 11)

**Sabine Hossenfelder.** ["The Problem with Quantum Measurement"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Be3HlA_9968) YouTube, 22 Oct. 2019, youtube.com/watch?v=Be3HlA_9968.

*Sabine Hossenfelder, a working physicist with no patience for shortcuts, explains why decoherence dresses up the problem without actually solving it. Same video as Collapse, which names the everyday term for the same open question.*

[]{#glNegentropy .anchor}**Negentropy ---** Schrödinger's phrase for the usable order a living thing takes in to maintain itself while exporting disorder to its surroundings. A scheme within the second law's accounts, not an exemption from them. (Chapter 17)

**PBS Space Time.** ["The Physics of Life (ft. It's Okay to be Smart & PBS Eons!)"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GcfLZSL7YGw) YouTube, 11 Apr. 2018, youtube.com/watch?v=GcfLZSL7YGw.

*Thirteen minutes of PBS Space Time watching a disorder-loving universe assemble something as fussy as you, negentropy unnamed but unmistakable.*

[]{#glNocloning .anchor}**No-cloning Theorem** --- The proof that no physical process can make a perfect copy of an unknown arbitrary quantum state. Moving a state, or re-preparing a known one, is still allowed. (Chapter 8)

**minutephysics.** ["The No Cloning Theorem"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=owPC60Ue0BE) YouTube, 27 Dec. 2016, youtube.com/watch?v=owPC60Ue0BE.

*The full no-cloning proof delivered at whiteboard speed --- ten minutes instead of a linear-algebra lecture, cat cartoons standing in for qubits.*

**No-communication theorem** --- The proof that entanglement's correlations cannot be used to send a message faster than light. Each local notebook looks random until the two are compared later, slowly. (Chapter 7)

**Veritasium.** ["Quantum Entanglement & Spooky Action at a Distance"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZuvK-od647c) YouTube, 12 Jan. 2015, youtube.com/watch?v=ZuvK-od647c.

*Nine minutes confirming that nothing was sent, which is the entire courtesy. Same video as Nonlocality; this entry is the no-message half.*

**No-deleting theorem** --- The counterpart of no-cloning: given two copies of an unknown quantum state, no process can delete one and leave a perfect blank without the missing information turning up somewhere else. The vault fails in both directions. (Chapter 8)

**minutephysics.** ["The No Cloning Theorem"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=owPC60Ue0BE) YouTube, 27 Dec. 2016, youtube.com/watch?v=owPC60Ue0BE.

*Same linearity that forbids copying forbids this kind of deletion; the film covers the copying half.*

[]{#glNonlocality .anchor}**Nonlocality** --- A dependence of what is true here on a distant configuration, with no signal passing between. Pilot-wave theory is openly nonlocal; Bell's theorem shows nature is not locally real. (Chapter 13)

**Veritasium.** ["Quantum Entanglement & Spooky Action at a Distance"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZuvK-od647c) YouTube, 12 Jan. 2015, youtube.com/watch?v=ZuvK-od647c.

*Nine minutes on the distant-correlation half of the same footage --- what changes on one side when nothing is touched on the other. Same video as No-communication theorem, which covers why no message can ride along.*

[]{#glNuclearShell .anchor}**Nuclear Shell Model --- The reason some isotopes are stable while their neighbours are not: protons and neutrons fill discrete nuclear energy shells, the same shell logic that organises electrons into atomic orbitals, just at nuclear rather than atomic energies.**

**DrPhysicsA.** ["The Nuclear Shell Model: An Introduction"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8vMwzkOi0v4) YouTube, Sept. 2012, youtube.com/watch?v=8vMwzkOi0v4.

*DrPhysicsA works the same shell-filling argument on a whiteboard, at lecture pace, deriving the magic numbers instead of just declaring them.*

**Page curve** --- The expected rise-then-fall of the entropy carried by a black hole's emitted radiation, if the information is preserved. Early radiation looks thermal; later radiation is correlated with it, so its entropy falls again as the correlations accumulate. (Chapter 14)

**PBS Space Time.** ["Have We SOLVED The Black Hole Information Paradox with Wormholes?"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=epSev7ovVew) YouTube, 15 June 2022, youtube.com/watch?v=epSev7ovVew.

*Already cited under the Information Problem; this is the curve's own name, for Lecture 14 to point at directly.*

[]{#glPilot .anchor}**Pilot-Wave Theory (de Broglie--Bohm)** --- An interpretation in which particles always have definite positions and are guided by a real, non-collapsing wave spread over every possible configuration. Deterministic and explicitly nonlocal. (Chapter 12)

**PBS Space Time.** ["Pilot Wave Theory and Quantum Realism \| Space Time \| PBS Digital Studios"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RlXdsyctD50) YouTube, 30 Nov. 2016, youtube.com/watch?v=RlXdsyctD50.

*The de Broglie--Bohm case at video speed --- twelve minutes, hosted by an astrophysicist who treats a non-collapsing wave as unremarkable.*

[]{#glPointer .anchor}**Pointer States** --- The robust, stable states --- typically well-defined positions --- that survive constant environmental monitoring and make up the everyday classical world. They are the states that survive being photocopied by the environment; superpositions of very different positions do not photocopy. (Chapter 10)

**PBS Space Time.** ["How Do Quantum States Manifest In The Classical World?"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vSnq5Hs3_wI) YouTube, 16 Mar. 2020, youtube.com/watch?v=vSnq5Hs3_wI.

*PBS Space Time's Matt O'Dowd explains why pointer states survive the environment's constant surveillance, unlike everything else quantum and interesting. Same video as Einselection and Quantum Darwinism; this entry is the what-survives half.*

**QBism --- An interpretation that treats the quantum state as an agent's own degree of belief about what they will experience next, not as an objective, mind-independent description of the world. (Chapter 11)**

*Use as a map of the options on the panel, not as an endorsement of any one of them.*

**Quantum Darwinism** --- The idea that especially robust states get copied redundantly into many parts of the environment, so that independent observers checking different fragments of it agree on the same outcome. The physical content behind a phrase like 'a widening region of agreement, spreading at the speed of gossip.' (Chapter 11)

**PBS Space Time.** ["How Do Quantum States Manifest In The Classical World?"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vSnq5Hs3_wI) YouTube, 16 Mar. 2020, youtube.com/watch?v=vSnq5Hs3_wI.

*Fourteen minutes of decoherence and redundant records, walked through by a host who lets the tensor products do the talking. Same video as Einselection and Pointer States; this entry is the many-observers-agree half.*

[]{#glQuantumErrorCorrection .anchor}**Quantum Error Correction** --- Protecting information by spreading it across many systems as a pattern, then checking for damage with syndrome measurements that locate errors without reading the protected data. Works below a threshold error rate, not above it. (Chapter 15)

**Google Quantum AI.** ["Demonstrating Quantum Error Correction"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_ugJLuJ1_gM) YouTube, 22 Feb. 2023, youtube.com/watch?v=\_ugJLuJ1_gM.

*The Nature paper compressed into four minutes, narrated by the physicists who actually ran the experiment and watched the errors shrink.*

**Quantum tunnelling --- A system's amplitude leaks through a barrier that classical energy accounting would call closed. Alpha decay, the scanning tunnelling microscope, and the Sun's fusion all depend on it. (Chapter 6 / Backlog)**

**PBS Space Time.** ["Is Quantum Tunneling Faster than Light?"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-IfmgyXs7z8) YouTube, 2 June 2016, youtube.com/watch?v=-IfmgyXs7z8.

*Also the honest answer: no, it is not a loophole in relativity.*

[]{#glQuantumZeno .anchor}**Quantum Zeno Effect --- The real, observed effect by which very frequent measurements of a quantum system can inhibit its normal change. Not a licence for anyone to freeze a thing by watching it. (Chapter 4)**

**PBS Space Time.** ["Zeno's Paradox & The Quantum Zeno Effect"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SMPid7Sh0EE) YouTube, 23 Mar. 2021, youtube.com/watch?v=SMPid7Sh0EE.

*Zeno's paradox, updated --- PBS Space Time spends a quarter hour on it, hosted by an astrophysicist who never blinks at the maths.*

**Relational quantum mechanics** --- Rovelli's account: facts about a system are relative to another system, not absolute. Wigner and his friend need not share one official list. (Chapter 11)

*Helgoland, already in Further Reading, is the book-length version --- Wigner's friend taken seriously instead of panicked about.*

[]{#glSchrdinger .anchor}**Schrödinger Equation** --- The rule governing how an isolated quantum state changes between measurements: smoothly, predictably and reversibly. The least mysterious part of the subject. (Chapter 5)

**Up and Atom.** ["What is The Schrödinger Equation, Exactly?"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QeUMFo8sODk) YouTube, 6 July 2018, youtube.com/watch?v=QeUMFo8sODk.

*Nine minutes on why the wavefunction glides smoothly until someone looks --- hosted by a channel with a visible fondness for the equation. Same video as Born rule, which covers where the amplitudes land.*

[]{#glSchrdingers .anchor}**Schrödinger's Cat ---** Schrödinger's 1935 thought experiment, meant as a reductio ad absurdum of an unexplained measurement process --- not a claim that a cat is literally half alive. (Chapter 18)

**TED-Ed.** ["Schrödinger's cat: A thought experiment in quantum mechanics - Chad Orzel"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UjaAxUO6-Uw) YouTube, 14 Oct. 2014, youtube.com/watch?v=UjaAxUO6-Uw.

*Schrödinger's reductio restaged as a four-minute animated lesson, scripted by a working physicist, cat's fate no clearer at the end.*

[]{#glSecond .anchor}**Second Law of Thermodynamics** --- Total entropy does not decrease. Local order can be built only by exporting greater disorder somewhere else. (Chapter 16)

**Veritasium.** ["The Most Misunderstood Concept in Physics"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DxL2HoqLbyA) YouTube, 1 July 2023, youtube.com/watch?v=DxL2HoqLbyA.

*The same argument at documentary length: twenty-seven minutes instead of two sentences, hosted by a communicator who treats entropy as physics' best plot twist.*

[]{#glState .anchor}**State (Quantum state)** --- The complete account physics can give of a system: what results its possible measurements can produce, and with what probabilities. Not a location, and not a hidden ledger of answers. (Chapter 1)

**MIT.** ["Quantum Explained"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jk0jWzlvA5w) YouTube, 10 June 2024, youtube.com/watch?v=jk0jWzlvA5w.

*Four brisk minutes from the director of MIT's quantum computing centre, laying out what a quantum state actually is before anyone starts complicating it.*

[]{#glStateReduction .anchor}**State Reduction --- The idealised update of a state to the outcome just recorded; an immediate repeat of the same measurement then returns the same result with certainty. See also Collapse, the everyday name for the same jump. (Chapter 4)**

**PBS Space Time.** ["Is The Wave Function The Building Block of Reality?"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FP6iyVJ70OU) YouTube, 16 Feb. 2022, youtube.com/watch?v=FP6iyVJ70OU.

*Twenty minutes on Objective Collapse Theories --- state reduction as real physics rather than metaphysics --- hosted by a working astrophysicist.*

[]{#glString .anchor}**String Theory --- The proposal that the basic objects of nature are tiny vibrating extended things rather than points, with different vibrations appearing as different particles. Five different, independently consistent versions were developed before physicists realised they were one theory seen five ways --- see Duality and M-theory. Mathematically rich, experimentally unconfirmed. (Chapter 14)**

**Kurzgesagt -- In a Nutshell.** ["String Theory Explained -- What is The True Nature of Reality?"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Da-2h2B4faU) YouTube, 1 Mar. 2018, youtube.com/watch?v=Da-2h2B4faU.

*The extra-dimensional pitch delivered in eight tidy animated minutes, narrated by a voice too polite to say whether it's true.*

[]{#glSuperposition .anchor}**Superposition** --- A quantum state that combines alternatives without secretly holding one definite answer to the question being asked; its alternatives can interfere. Not "two places at once" in the naive sense. (Chapter 1)

**Up and Atom.** ["Superposition of Quantum States"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VwWRX9IdblE) YouTube, 9 Aug. 2018, youtube.com/watch?v=VwWRX9IdblE.

*Thirteen minutes of Stern-Gerlach magnets, for readers who want spin sitting in two states at once shown, not just asserted.*

[]{#glSyndrome .anchor}**Syndrome Measurement** --- A measurement, in quantum error correction, that reveals which kind of damage has occurred without reading out, and so without disturbing, the protected logical state itself. (Chapter 15)

**Qiskit.** ["Quantum Error Correction \| Understanding Quantum Information & Computation \| Lesson 15"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9TCIOm8gcVQ) YouTube, 8 Apr. 2025, youtube.com/watch?v=9TCIOm8gcVQ.

*Forty-six unhurried minutes from an actual professor, syndromes included, delivered with the calm of someone who grades exams for a living.*

[]{#glTsirelson .anchor}**Tsirelson Bound** --- The ceiling quantum mechanics puts on its own Bell (CHSH) correlations: S ≤ 2√2 ≈ 2.83 --- above the local-realistic limit of 2, but not unlimited. (Chapter 13)

**Mark Wilde.** ["Lecture 14: Quantum Information Theory - Fall 2021 - Mark Wilde"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IgzMNjVQVnc) YouTube, 24 Oct. 2021, youtube.com/watch?v=IgzMNjVQVnc.

*The Bell inequality story taught properly: eighty minutes of blackboard rigor from the physicist who wrote the textbook on quantum information theory.*

[]{#glUncertainty .anchor}**Uncertainty Principle** --- The intrinsic limit that a quantum system cannot be prepared with arbitrarily sharp values of both position and momentum at once: Δx·Δp ≥ ℏ/2. A fact about states, not about instruments. (Chapter 9)

**TED-Ed.** ["What is the Heisenberg Uncertainty Principle? - Chad Orzel"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=TQKELOE9eY4) YouTube, 2017, youtube.com/watch?v=TQKELOE9eY4.

*TED-Ed compresses the whole argument into a few animated minutes, narrated by a working physicist who resists shrugging and saying 'it's complicated.'*

[]{#glUnitary .anchor}**Unitary Evolution** --- The technical name for the smooth, reversible, probability-preserving change a state undergoes under the Schrödinger equation. (Chapter 5)

**PBS Space Time.** ["Why Quantum Information is Never Destroyed"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HF-9Dy6iB_4) YouTube, 23 May 2018, youtube.com/watch?v=HF-9Dy6iB_4.

*Fourteen minutes on unitarity, from a physicist who apologises for the maths but uses it anyway.*

**Unruh effect** --- An accelerating observer can find a warm bath in a vacuum that an inertial observer calls empty. Cousin to Hawking radiation; a different reason to distrust the word "nothing." (Chapter 6)

**PBS Space Time.** ["The Unruh Effect"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7cj6oiFDEXc) YouTube, 4 Apr. 2018, youtube.com/watch?v=7cj6oiFDEXc.

*Eleven minutes on why an accelerating thermometer can find warmth in a vacuum another observer still calls empty.*

[]{#glWigners .anchor}**Wigner's Friend** --- A thought experiment in which an observer inside a sealed laboratory records a definite result while an observer outside must still describe the whole laboratory as one unresolved quantum system. It sharpens the measurement problem. (Chapter 11)

**Sabine Hossenfelder.** ["Has quantum mechanics proved that reality does not exist?"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Wsjgtp9XZxo) YouTube, 19 Feb. 2022, youtube.com/watch?v=Wsjgtp9XZxo.

*Eleven brisk minutes from a working physicist calmly dismantling the headlines that quantum mechanics had abolished objective reality, Wigner's friend and all.*

# **Further Reading**

After the last lecture --- what you could actually read without losing it

Al-Khalili, Jim. Quantum: A Guide for the Perplexed. Weidenfeld & Nicolson, 2003.

*Friendly without being false. A spare on-ramp if Gribbin is already on the shelf.*

[Barbour, Julian](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GoTeGW2csPk)**.** The End of Time: The Next Revolution in Physics. Oxford University Press, 1999.

*Time as a change in the configuration of the world. A challenging read.*

Becker, Adam. What Is Real? Basic Books, 2018.

*A history of the measurement fight that does not pretend Copenhagen won by knockout. Useful before Chapter 11.*

Bekenstein, Jacob D. "Black Holes and Entropy." Physical Review D, vol. 7, no. 8, 1973, pp. 2333--2346.

*The area law before Hawking finished the temperature. Short, historic, and the reason 4C was never a cupboard.*

Bell, J. S. Speakable and Unspeakable in Quantum Mechanics. 2nd ed., Cambridge University Press, 2004.

*The papers themselves, including the one that ends local innocence. Bellboy, without the Belfast jokes.*

Bohm, David. "A Suggested Interpretation of the Quantum Theory in Terms of 'Hidden' Variables, I & II." Physical Review, vol. 85, no. 2, 1952, pp. 166--193.

*The Duc's mathematics without the gloves. Not light reading. Entirely optional.*

[]{#frCarrollSomethingDeeplyHidden .anchor}[Carroll, Sean](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XBQorAnnICs)**.** Something Deeply Hidden: Quantum Worlds and the Emergence of Spacetime. Dutton, 2019.

*The many-worlds refusal to collapse anything at all. Written so the argument can be followed.*

[Feynman, Richard P.](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kEx-gRfuhhk) The Character of Physical Law. MIT Press, 1967.

*What a physical law is allowed to look like. Told without hiding behind the formalism.*

[Feynman, Richard P.](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bAnnEiAjN6U) QED: The Strange Theory of Light and Matter. Princeton University Press, 1985.

*Little arrows instead of a pile of operators. Light, matter, and the diagrams that produced the calculation look like a story. Highly recommended.*

[Gamow, George](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WIKOFLRQuc8)**.** Mr Tompkins in Wonderland. Cambridge University Press, 1940.

*A physicist shrinks the speed of light and Planck's constant down to walking pace, so tunnelling through a wall looks as ordinary as it apparently is. The oldest trick in this list, and still the friendliest.*

[Gell-Mann, Murray](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jKFZSzJMqpg)**.** The Quark and the Jaguar: Adventures in the Simple and the Complex. W. H. Freeman, 1994.

*The man who named the quark, writing about complexity, emergence, and why simple rules produce a complicated universe. He mentions Joyce.*

[Greene, Brian](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YtdE662eY_M)**.** The Elegant Universe: Superstrings, Hidden Dimensions, and the Quest for the Ultimate Theory. W. W. Norton, 1999.

*A nod to strings and extra dimensions in a form you can actually finish.*

[Gribbin, John](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8V669ohFOkI)**. In Search of Schrödinger's Cat: Quantum Physics and Reality. Bantam Books, 1984.**

*The book that made the cat a celebrity outside physics departments --- minimal maths, plenty of explanation, and reassuringly no live-dead ambiguity left by the last page.*

[Griffiths, David J.](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FY076-fEZ-s)**, and Darrell F. Schroeter.** Introduction to Quantum Mechanics. 3rd ed., Cambridge University Press, 2018.

*A clear and widely used undergraduate introduction to quantum mechanics, balancing mathematical rigor with physical intuition. Not an easy read though.*

[Hawking, Stephen W.](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EPNGZpp6Glo) A Brief History of Time. Bantam Books, 1988.

*Black holes radiate, and entropy proportional to the area of the horizon rather than the volume. Great read.*

[Hawking, Stephen](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5hVmeOCJjOU)**, ed.** The Dreams That Stuff Is Made Of: The Most Astounding Papers of Quantum Physics---and How They Shook the Scientific World. Running Press, 2011.

*The original papers themselves, with commentary --- including the quiet, still-unexplained mystery of the fine-structure constant this book keeps circling back to without ever quite landing on.*

[Heisenberg, Werner](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xbpOMkBMtYU)**.** Physics and Philosophy: The Revolution in Modern Science. Harper & Row, 1958.

*Uncertainty as a feature of physical states, not merely a consequence of clumsy observation.*

[McFadden, Johnjoe](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kZY0lhgZC0c)**, and Jim Al-Khalili.** Life on the Edge: The Coming of Age of Quantum Biology. Crown, 2015.

*An accessible introduction to quantum biology, exploring whether living systems exploit quantum effects and what that might mean for understanding life itself.*

Nielsen, Michael A., and Isaac L. Chuang. Quantum Computation and Quantum Information. Cambridge University Press, 2010.

*The standard door into no-cloning, syndromes, and thresholds. Lecture 8 and Lecture 15's grown-up cousin.*

Penrose, Roger. The Emperor's New Mind. Oxford University Press, 1989.

*Wrong in places that matter, honest in places that matter more. Included because the book already refuses tidy answers, not because it endorses Orch-OR.*

Preskill, John. Lecture notes on Quantum Computation. Caltech, various years. Freely available at preskill.caltech.edu/ph219/.

*Clearer than a Ministry circular, and updated when the facts move. Use for error correction, and for the sentence "this is not settled."*

[]{#frRovelliHelgoland .anchor}[Rovelli, Carlo](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gpWf2wyGQ0Q)**.** Helgoland: Making Sense of the Quantum Revolution. Riverhead Books, 2021.

*Wigner's friend taken seriously: what counts as a measurement depends on who you ask, and the book makes peace with that instead of panicking about it.*

[Schrödinger, Erwin](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=uGGfmZGgoAE)**.** What Is Life? The Physical Aspect of the Living Cell. Cambridge University Press, 1944.

*How a living cell stays so orderly in a world that runs towards disorder. Schrödinger's answers were hereditary information stored in an "aperiodic crystal" and life's ability to feed from its environment.*

Susskind, Leonard. The Black Hole War. Little, Brown, 2008.

*Holography as an argument people actually had. Pair with Lecture 14 Part 2.*

Wallace, David. The Emergent Multiverse. Oxford University Press, 2012.

*The most careful philosophical defence of Everett. Tengelman would like it. Mrs Chain would ask who pays for the other branches.*

[Wheeler, John Archibald](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SsBACBVtPYs)**, with Kenneth Ford.** Geons, Black Holes, and Quantum Foam: A Life in Physics. W. W. Norton & Company, 1998.

*Wheeler's own memoir, told as a life rather than a lecture --- black holes, wormholes, and where 'it from bit' actually came from. The friendliest way in.*

[Zurek, Wojciech H.](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vSnq5Hs3_wI) Quantum Darwinism. Nature Physics, vol. 5, 2009, pp. 181--188.

*https://arxiv.org/pdf/0903.5082*

*The paper that gives 'a widening region of agreement, spreading at the speed of gossip' its real name: why the everyday world looks so solidly agreed-upon to anyone who checks.*

**PBS Space Time.** ["How Do Quantum States Manifest In The Classical World?"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vSnq5Hs3_wI) YouTube, 16 Mar. 2020, youtube.com/watch?v=vSnq5Hs3_wI.

*Fourteen minutes standing in for nine pages of Zurek, from a working physicist who does not flinch at the equations.*

# **A Word on Who Is Who**

This novel gives away real physicists twice over, by two entirely different methods.

Characters in this book say the names of real scientists without the faintest idea who they are. A woman whose crockery has been administratively altered will demand to know who Hawking is, and be told, correctly, that he is a physicist and not a department.

The second method is quieter, and if you enjoy working things out before you are told, stop reading this page now and come back when you have finished the book. Nothing here spoils the plot. It does spoil a run of small pleasures.

The consultants are some of the greatest physicists of the twentieth century, lightly disguised, mildly inconvenienced, and required to explain themselves to a Third Grade officer with a green notebook.

They are not caricatures of the real people and their private lives here are wholly invented --- but their physics is theirs.

+-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+-------------------+----------------------------------------+
| **In the novel**                                                                                          | **In reality**    | **What they explain here**             |
+:=========================================================================================================:+===================+========================================+
| Albrecht Eilstein --- courteous, dishevelled, and a vampire who feeds on collapsed and discarded branches | Albert Einstein   | The measurement problem;               |
|                                                                                                           |                   |                                        |
|                                                                                                           |                   | Wigner's friend;                       |
|                                                                                                           |                   |                                        |
|                                                                                                           |                   | why he will not accept the tidy answer |
+-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+-------------------+----------------------------------------+
| The Duc de Broccoli --- explains himself very slowly indeed                                               | Louis de Broglie  | Matter waves; pilot-wave theory;       |
|                                                                                                           |                   |                                        |
|                                                                                                           |                   | determinism at the cost of locality    |
+-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+-------------------+----------------------------------------+
| Werner Heisenburger --- declines to speculate about anything he cannot measure                            | Werner Heisenberg | Operationalism;                        |
|                                                                                                           |                   |                                        |
|                                                                                                           |                   | the Copenhagen position                |
+-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+-------------------+----------------------------------------+
| Max Tengelman --- cheerfully believes in all of it, all at once                                           | Max Tegmark       | Many worlds;                           |
|                                                                                                           |                   |                                        |
|                                                                                                           |                   | the mathematical universe              |
+-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+-------------------+----------------------------------------+
| Jack Bellboy --- deceased, of Belfast, and still the most dangerous man in the room                       | John S. Bell      | Bell's Theorem;                        |
|                                                                                                           |                   |                                        |
|                                                                                                           |                   | the end of local hidden variables      |
+-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+-------------------+----------------------------------------+
| Eduard von Wittenberg --- 11 dimensions and no experimental confirmation whatsoever                       | Edward Witten     | String theory;                         |
|                                                                                                           |                   |                                        |
|                                                                                                           |                   | M-theory;                              |
|                                                                                                           |                   |                                        |
|                                                                                                           |                   | black-hole entropy; holography         |
+-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+-------------------+----------------------------------------+
| Erich Schrottfinger --- has views about how his cat has been reported                                     | Erwin Schrödinger | What living things actually do;        |
|                                                                                                           |                   |                                        |
|                                                                                                           |                   | the long future                        |
+-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+-------------------+----------------------------------------+

No disrespect is intended to any of them, living or dead. All of them did far more for human understanding than anyone in this book manages, and not one of them, so far as the author has been able to establish, was ever required to complete a Form 4C in triplicate.

# **A Note on the Author**

**Quentin Edmund Darling (Q.E.D.)** is a pseudonym. It stands, depending on whom you ask,

for

**Quod Erat Demonstrandum --- Latin for "which was to be demonstrated"**

or for

***Quantum ElectroDynamics*** --- the theory that finally taught physics to be honest about how thoroughly strange light and matter actually are.

Both readings are correct. That was rather the point of the book you have just finished.

The name behind the pseudonym is

**Lothar J. Musiol**

Mr Musiol spent decades in the electronics industry: the kind of career that leaves a person fluent in semiconductors, RF, and the precise moment at which an engineering deadline becomes a philosophical position.

Before that he studied physics seriously enough to know exactly how much of it he is simplifying here for comic effect, and exactly how much he is not.

Readers who found the bra-ket notation in Lolly Wren's green notebook oddly natural instead of decorative may draw their own conclusions.

He wrote *Schrödinger's Paperwork* the way Lolly Wren does most things --- carefully, in the margins of a life spent elsewhere, and with the slowly hardening suspicion that the paperwork was more interesting than anyone had given it credit for.

He is German by birth, and chooses the almost-right official word on purpose, which may explain the book's affection for language that is careful, exact, and very slightly in the wrong register.

# **About the Series**

**Schrödinger's Paperwork** is Book 1 of *Lolly Wren's Curious Science Adventures*, a series in which large institutions attempt to administer physics, and physics declines to cooperate.

Each book stands alone. Each teaches its own set of ideas honestly, in the same two-layer form: a comic novel that never stops to lecture, and clearly separated lectures for readers who want the real science and are prepared to be told when it is unsettled.

Book 2 is already waiting: a different story with a different arrangement of collapsing certainties --- for readers who did not, in the event, get enough the first time. It also inherits what still didn't fit: [nuclear shells](#glNuclearShell), and everything else that never made it past a glossary entry, are now, appropriately, somebody else's problem.

Look for the next Lolly Wren adventure wherever you found this one.

# **If This Made You Curious**

None of the physics in this book was invented for the joke. If you would like to go further than a Ministry can take you, three places are worth the trip --- two of them already named above, one not:

Sean Carroll's [*Something Deeply Hidden*](#frCarrollSomethingDeeplyHidden) takes many-worlds seriously and argues for it properly.

Carlo Rovelli's [Helgoland](#frRovelliHelgoland) makes the case for relational quantum mechanics in prose good enough to make you forgive him for being persuasive. And if you want the same territory covered at considerably greater length and with far less bureaucracy,

*Kenneth Ford's The Quantum World goes much further into most of the questions this book only had time to dramatise.*

# **If You Enjoyed This Book**

Reviews are how small books find their readers. If *Schrödinger's Paperwork* made you laugh, or helped you understand something you had always been told was impossible to grasp, a few honest sentences on the store page will do more good than the author ever could alone.

And if you want more paperwork failing at physics, Book 2 of *Lolly Wren's Curious Science Adventures* is already sharpening its pencils.

If the book also made you look differently at an official letter, at the sentence that quietly moves a difficulty somewhere you cannot quite see, then it has done its real work. In that case, you may consider the notebook successfully passed on.

*There is no lid.*
